}\n204. \n205. func TestConfig(t *testing.T) {\n206. \tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n207. \tdefer cancel()\n208. \n209. \tvar mockACME http.Handler\n210. \tsrv := *testing.T) {\n300. \tli, err := net.Listen(\"tcp\", \"127.0.0.1:0\")\n301. \tif !assert.NoError(t, err) {\n302. \t\treturn\n303. \t}\n304. \taddr := li.Addr().String()\n305. \t_ = li.Close()\n306. \n307. \tsrc := config.NewStaticSource(&config.Config{\n308. \t\tOptions: &config.Options{\n309. \t\t\tHTTPRedirectAddr: addr,\n310. \t\t\tSetResponseHeaders: map[string]string{\n311. \t\t\tconn.Close()\n352. \t\t\treturn nil\n353. \t\t}\n354. \t\ttime.Sleep(time.Second)\n355. \t}\n356. \treturn err\n357. }\n358. \n359. func readJWSPayload(r io.Reader, dst interface{}) {\n360. Here’s a summary of their relevance in this version:\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **CertMagic v0.16.0 Overview**\\n- [CertMagic v0.16.0](https://github.com/caddyserver/certmagic/releases/tag/v0.16.0) is a Go library for automated TLS certificate management.\\n- The library underwent **breaking changes** in v0.16.0 to more fully separate CA/issuer logic from internal ACME operations.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## `ACMEManager` in v0.16.0\\n\\n### **What is ACMEManager?**\\n- `ACMEManager` was previously responsible for *all* ACME protocol operations: account registration, certificate issuance, renewal, and so forth.\\n- **In v0.16.0, much of this logic was migrated to \\\"issuers\\\"** to support new certificate authorities and to better organize cert management.\\n\\n### **Status in v0.16.0**\\n- `ACMEManager` became mostly a legacy type, with its responsibilities divided or delegated.\\n- The **preferred interface** for ACME operations became the `Issuer` abstraction.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## `ACMEIssuer` in v0.16.0\\n\\n### **What is ACMEIssuer?**\\n- Introduced in CertMagic v0.16.0 as part of the new issuer system.\\n- Responsible specifically for interacting with ACME CAs (e.g., Let's Encrypt, ZeroSSL).\\n- Implements the new `Issuer` interface, which allows for different certificate sources (e.g., internal, local, or custom CAs).\\n\\n#### **Key role:**\\n- `ACMEIssuer` **replaces** (or wraps) most of what `ACMEManager` did in prior versions.\\n- You construct an `ACMEIssuer`, configure it (with your ACME directory, email, challenges, etc.), and use it as the certificate source.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **Relationship**\\n- **Pre-v0.16**: Use `ACMEManager`.\\n- **v0.16+**: Use `ACMEIssuer` via the new issuer framework — don’t use `ACMEManager` directly for new code.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **Example**: Usage in v0.16.0\\n\\n```go\\nimport \\\"github.com/caddyserver/certmagic\\\"\\n\\n// Create an ACME issuer\\nacmeIssuer := certmagic.NewACMEIssuer(certmagic.ACMEIssuer{\\n    CA:    certmagic.LetsEncryptProductionCA,\\n    Email: \\\"your-email@example.com\\\",\\n    // ...other options...\\n})\\n\\n// Create a CertMagic Config using this issuer\\ncfg := certmagic.NewDefault()\\ncfg.Issuers = []certmagic.Issuer{acmeIssuer}\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **References**\\n- [CertMagic v0.16.0 Release `ACMEIssuer` in v0.16.0\\n\\n### **What is ACMEIssuer?**\\n- Introduced in CertMagic v0.16.0 as part of the new issuer system.\\n- Responsible specifically for interacting with ACME CAs (e.g., Let's Encrypt, ZeroSSL).\\n- Implements the new `Issuer` interface, which allows for different certificate sources (e.g., internal, local, or custom CAs).\\n\\n#### **Key role:**\\n- `ACMEIssuer` **replaces** (or wraps) most of what `ACMEManager` did in prior versions.\\n- You construct an `ACMEIssuer`, configure it (with your ACME directory, email, challenges, etc.), and use it as the certificate source.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **Relationship**\\n- **Pre-v0.16**: Use `ACMEManager`.\\n- **v0.16+**: Use `ACMEIssuer` via the new issuer framework — don’t use `ACMEManager` directly for new code.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **Example**: Usage in v0.16.0\\n\\n```go\\nimport \\\"github.com/caddyserver/certmagic\\\"\\n\\n// Create an ACME issuer\\nacmeIssuer := certmagic.NewACMEIssuer(certmagic.ACMEIssuer{\\n    CA:    certmagic.LetsEncryptProductionCA,\\n    Email: \\\"your-email@example.com\\\",\\n    // ...other options...\\n})\\n\\n// Create a CertMagic Config using this issuer\\ncfg := certmagic.NewDefault()\\ncfg.Issuers = []certmagic.Issuer{acmeIssuer}\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **References**\\n- [CertMagic v0.16.0 Release Notes](https://github.com/caddyserver/certmagic/releases/tag/v0.16.0)\\n- [ACMEIssuer Documentation (Godoc)](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/caddyserver/certmagic@v0.16.0#ACMEIssuer)\\n\\n---\\n\\n**Summary:**  \\nIn v0.16.0, use `ACMEIssuer` (not `ACMEManager`) for ACME certificate tasks. `ACMEManager` is now mostly legacy/internal, while `ACMEIssuer` is the public API you should use for handling ACME protocol certificates in v0.16.0 (or v0.16)**, the `ACMEManager` and `ACMEIssuer` types play key roles in managing ACME (Automatic Certificate Management Environment) certificates, such as those from Let's Encrypt. Here’s a summary of their relevance in this version:\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **CertMagic v0.16.0 Overview**\\n- [CertMagic v0.16.0](https://github.com/caddyserver/certmagic/releases/tag/v0.16.0) is a Go library for automated TLS certificate management.\\n- The library underwent **breaking changes** in v0.16.0 to more fully separate CA/issuer logic from internal ACME operations.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## `ACMEManager` in v0.16.0\\n\\n### **What is ACMEManager?**\\n- `ACMEManager` was previously responsible for *all* ACME protocol operations: account registration, certificate issuance, renewal, and so forth.\\n- **In v0.16.0, much of this logic was migrated to \\\"issuers\\\"** to support new certificate authorities and to better organize cert management.\\n\\n### **Status in v0.16.0**\\n- `ACMEManager` became mostly a legacy type, with its responsibilities divided or delegated.\\n- The **preferred interface** for ACME operations became the `Issuer` abstraction.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## `ACMEIssuer` in v0.16.0\\n\\n### **What is ACMEIssuer?**\\n- Introduced in CertMagic v0.16.0 as part of the new issuer system.\\n- Responsible specifically for interacting with ACME CAs (e.g., Let's Encrypt, ZeroSSL).\\n- Implements the new `Issuer` interface, which allows for different certificate sources (e.g., internal, local, or custom CAs).\\n\\n#### **Key role:**\\n- `ACMEIssuer` **replaces** (or wraps) most of what `ACMEManager` did in prior versions.\\n- You construct an `ACMEIssuer`, configure it (with your ACME directory, email, challenges, etc.), and use it as the certificate source.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **Relationship**\\n- **Pre-v0.16**: Use `ACMEManager`.\\n- **v0.16+**: Use `ACMEIssuer` via the new issuer framework — don’t use `ACMEManager` directly for new code.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **Example**: Usage in v0.16.0\\n\\n```go\\nimport \\\"github.com/caddyserver/certmagic\\\"\\n\\n// Create an ACME issuer\\nacmeIssuer := certmagic.NewACMEIssuer(certmagic.ACMEIssuer{\\n    CA:    certmagic.LetsEncryptProductionCA,\\n    Email: \\\"your-email@example.com\\\",\\n    // ...other options...\\n})\\n\\n// Create a CertMagic Config using this issuer\\ncfg := certmagic.NewDefault()\\ncfg.Issuers = []certmagic.Issuer{acmeIssuer}\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **References**\\n- [CertMagic v0.16.0 Release Notes](https://github.com/caddyserver/certmagic/releases/tag/v0.16.0)\\n- [ACMEIssuer Documentation (Godoc)](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/caddyserver/certmagic@v0.16.0#ACMEIssuer)\\n\\n---\\n\\n**Summary:**  \\nIn v0.16.0, use `ACMEIssuer` (not `ACMEManager`) for ACME certificate tasks.
